Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the oscillations structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 386. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 801 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to oscillations theory, we decompose the problem into 386 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 801. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
